= 4500;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n    const starColors = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n\n    const Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const color = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];\n      starColors[i * 3] = color.r;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = color.g;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;\n    }\n\n '一時停止' : '再生'}\n          aria-pressed={settings.isPlaying}\n        >\n          {settings.isPlaying ? '⏸ 一時停止' : '▶ 再生'}\n        </button>\n\n        <button\n          className={`btn ${settings.showOrbits ? data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { SimulationSettings } from './types';\nimport { getCelestialBodyById } from './solarSystemData';\nimport SolarSystemCanvas from './SolarSystemCanvas';\nimport ControlPanel from './ControlPanel';\nimport PlanetDetailCard from './PlanetDetailCard';\nimport './index.css';\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SimulationSettings>({\n    isPlaying: true,\n    speedMultiplier: 1.0,\n    showOrbits: true,\n    focusedPlanetId: null\n  });\n\n  // 惑星選択・フォーカス切り替えハンドラー\n  const handleSelectPlanet = useCallback((id: string | null) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      focusedPlanetId: id as SimulationSettings['focusedPlanetId']\n    }));\n  }, []);\n\n  // グローバルキーボードショートカット (Space: 再生/一時停止トグル, Escape: 全景リセット)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      // 入力要素にフォーカスがある場合は無視\n      if (\n        e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement ||\n        e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement ||\n        e.target instanceof HTMLSelectElement\n      ) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          isPlaying: !prev.isPlaying\n        }));\n      } else if (e.code === 'Escape') {\n        handleSelectPlanet(null);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [handleSelectPlanet]);\n\n  // 選択された天体のデータ取得\n  const selectedPlanet = settings.focusedPlanetId\n    ? onSelectPlanet: (id: string | null) => void;\n}\n\ninterface PlanetMeshEntry {\n  body: CelestialBody;\n  pivot: THREE.Group;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  glowMesh?: THREE.Mesh;\n  orbitLine?: THREE.Line;\n  moonGroup?: THREE.Group;\n  moonMesh?: THREE.Mesh;\n  moonOrbitLine?: THREE.Line;\n}\n\nexport default function SolarSystemCanvas({\n  settings,\n  onSelectPlanet\n}: SolarSystemCanvasProps) {\n  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  // Three.jsインスタンス参照\n  const sceneRef = useRef<THREE.Scene | null>(null);\n  const cameraRef = useRef<THREE.PerspectiveCamera | null>(null);\n  const rendererRef = useRef<THREE.WebGLRenderer | null>(null);\n  const planetEntriesRef = useRef<PlanetMeshEntry[]>([]);\n  const sunLightRef = useRef<THREE.PointLight | null>(null);\n\n  // カメラ操作・状態管理用Ref\n  const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);\n  const pointerDownPosRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n  const mousePosRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n  const cameraSphericalRef = useRef({\n    radius: 140,\n    theta: Math.PI / 4,\n    phi: Math.PI / 3\n  });\n  const cameraTargetRef = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0));\n  const currentCameraTargetRef = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0));\n\n  // 最新のPropsを保持\n  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n    // 軌道ラインの表示非表示を即座に更新\n    planetEntriesRef.current.forEach((entry) => {\n      if (entry.orbitLine) {\n        entry.orbitLine.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n      }\n    });\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = mountRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    // 1. シーン作成\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    sceneRef.current = scene;\n    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x030712, 0.0018);\n\n    // 2. カメラ作成\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(\n      45,\n      container.clientWidth / container.clientHeight,\n      0.1,\n      2000\n    );\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n    camera.position.set(0, 80, 120);\n\n    // 3. レンダラー作成\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });\n    rendererRef.current = renderer;\n    renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // 4. 環境光・太陽光\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x223344, 0.6);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff7e6, 3.5, 600, 0.5);\n    sunLight.position.set(0, 0, 0);\n    scene.add(sunLight);\n    sunLightRef.current = sunLight;\n\n    // 5. 星屑（Starfield背景パーティクル）\n    const starsCount = 4500;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n    const starColors = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n\n    const palette = [\n      new THREE.Color(0xffffff),\n      new THREE.Color(0x93c5fd),\n      new THREE.Color(0xfef08a),\n      new THREE.Color(0xf472b6)\n    ];\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {\n      const radius = 600 + Math.random() * 500;\n      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const phi = Math.acos(Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const color = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];\n      starColors[i * 3] = color.r;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = color.g;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.6,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85\n    });\n    const starField = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);\n    scene.add(starField);\n\n    // 6. 天体・軌道・リング生成\n    const entries: PlanetMeshEntry[] = [];\n\n    solarSystemData.forEach((body) => {\n      const pivot = new THREE.Group();\n      scene.add(pivot);\n\n      let glowMesh: THREE.Mesh | undefined;\n      const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius, 32, 32);\n      let sphereMat: THREE.Material;\n\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        sphereMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color)\n        });\n\n        const glowGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius * 1.25, 32, 32);\n        const glowMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.emissive || '#ff6600'),\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.28,\n          side: THREE.BackSide\n        });\n        glowMesh = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeo, glowMat);\n        pivot.add(glowMesh);\n      } else {\n        sphereMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color),\n          roughness: body.roughness,\n          metalness: 0.1\n        });\n      }\n\n      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);\n      mesh.position.set(body.orbitRadius, 0, 0);\n      mesh.userData = { bodyId: body.id, bodyData: body };\n      pivot.add(mesh);\n\n      // 惑星の傾き\n      if (body.id === 'earth') {\n        mesh.rotation.z = (23.4 * Math.PI) / 180;\n      } else if (body.id === 'uranus') {\n        mesh.rotation.z = (97.8 * Math.PI) / 180;\n      }\n\n      // 軌道ラインの生成\n      let orbitLine: THREE.Line | undefined;\n      if (body.orbitRadius > 0) {\n        const segments = 128;\n        const lineGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n        const linePositions = new Float32Array((segments + 1) * 3);\n\n        for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {\n          const theta = (i / segments) * Math.PI * 2;\n          linePositions[i * 3] = Math.cos(theta) * body.orbitRadius;\n          linePositions[i * 3 + 1] = 0;\n          linePositions[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(theta) * body.orbitRadius;\n        }\n\n        lineGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(linePositions, 3));\n        const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n          color: 0x38bdf8,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.22\n        });\n        orbitLine = new THREE.Line(lineGeo, lineMat);\n        orbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n        scene.add(orbitLine);\n      }\n\n      // 土星・天王星のリング\n      if (body.rings) {\n        const ringGeo = new
